commit fb5cbdb8c04fc21a75085e900765ce0a4046e43c
Author:     Hillel Gershuni <gershuni@gmail.com>
AuthorDate: Wed May 27 08:01:53 2026 +0300
Commit:     Hillel Gershuni <gershuni@gmail.com>
CommitDate: Wed May 27 08:01:53 2026 +0300

    fix(my-library): post-Codex review — LAB rebuild replace-not-append + double-click locator
    
    Follow-up to e397ad30 from the 2026-05-27 Codex review (97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX).
    
    Finding 1 (HIGH) — build_lab_side_index reuses the LOCAL LAB dir and only
    add_document'd rows, so a rebuild (now reachable after e397ad30 fixed the
    crash) appended duplicates and left deleted pages searchable while writing a
    "fresh" weights_hash. Added writer.delete_all_documents() before the add loop
    so a rebuild is a true replace, committed as one transaction. New regression
    test test_lab_rebuild_is_replace_not_append asserts LAB doc count == page count
    after two rebuilds (verified to fail without the fix — existing tests only
    checked .meta.json and SQLite local_pages, never the LAB Tantivy doc count).
    
    Finding 3 (MEDIUM) — double-click show_full_text() used currentRow() as an
    index into _collect_sorted_results(), which drops hidden (filtered) rows, so it
    could open the wrong result while the 👁 button (fixed in e397ad30) opened the
    right one. Now reads the clicked row's own result dict from COL_SYS_ID UserRole
    and delegates to show_full_text_for_result(), so both viewer paths resolve
    position by uid.
    
    Finding 2 (HIGH, deferred) — SearchEngine's LAB weights-hash differs from the
    LabEngine hash the rebuild writes, so standard Composition Search omits LOCAL
    LAB hits. Pre-existing (CR-01), never ran in production, LAB-mode unaffected;
    logged in docs/OPEN_ISSUES.md per user (LAB barely used).
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

         row = self.results_table.currentRow()
         if row < 0: return
 
-        sorted_results = self._collect_sorted_results()
-        if not sorted_results:
+        # currentRow() indexes the FULL table, but _collect_sorted_results() drops
+        # hidden (filtered) rows — using row as an index into that list opens the
+        # wrong result when any earlier row is hidden. Read the clicked row's own
+        # result dict and delegate to the uid-locator so this path stays consistent
+        # with the 👁 "View result" button (both resolve position by uid).
+        item = self.results_table.item(row, self.COL_SYS_ID)
+        res = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) if item else None
+        if not res:
             return
-        if row >= len(sorted_results):
-            row = 0
-        res = sorted_results[row]
         # Phase 95 smoke-fix (E): LOCAL double-click → ResultDialog (shows text).
         # The Browse panel path was removed because Browse does not render file text
         # correctly for LOCAL hits. ResultDialog already renders full_text which is
         # populated for LOCAL hits by _build_local_result_dict. The "Open file"
         # button is injected into ResultDialog by _inject_open_file_btn_into_rd()
         # so the user can still launch the source file from there.
-        ResultDialog(self, sorted_results, row, self.meta_mgr, self.searcher).exec()
+        self.show_full_text_for_result(res)
 
     def show_full_text_for_result(self, res):
         if not res:
diff --git a/shared/local_indexer.py b/shared/local_indexer.py
index ec7dc71a..b5b260d5 100644
--- a/shared/local_indexer.py
+++ b/shared/local_indexer.py
@@ -3535,6 +3535,13 @@ class LocalIndexer:
             pass
 
         writer = lab_index.writer(heap_size=50_000_000)
+        # True rebuild, not append: the LAB dir is reused across rebuilds
+        # (tantivy.Index reuse=True). Without clearing first, a rebuild after a
+        # weight change or a page deletion would leave stale fingerprint_dyn docs
+        # (and removed pages) beside the new rows, yet write a "fresh" weights_hash
+        # — duplicate/ghost LAB hits. delete_all_documents() + the add loop commit
+        # as one transaction, so only the current rows survive.
+        writer.delete_all_documents()
 
         rows_written = 0
         for sys_id, uid, page_num, file_id, content in self._iterate_lab_source_rows():
diff --git a/tests/test_local_lab_invalidation.py b/tests/test_local_lab_invalidation.py
index 54d3069a..edba708f 100644
--- a/tests/test_local_lab_invalidation.py
+++ b/tests/test_local_lab_invalidation.py
@@ -317,6 +317,53 @@ class TestBuildLabSideIndex:
         finally:
             indexer.close()
 
+    def test_lab_rebuild_is_replace_not_append(self, tmp_path):
+        """Codex 2026-05-27 HIGH: build_lab_side_index reuses the LAB dir, so a
+        rebuild must CLEAR existing docs first (delete_all_documents) — not append.
+        Without that, each rebuild duplicates every page (and keeps deleted pages)
+        while writing a 'fresh' weights_hash. Assert the LAB Tantivy doc count
+        equals the page count after a second rebuild, not twice it.
+        """
+        try:
+            import tantivy  # noqa
+        except ImportError:
+            pytest.skip("tantivy not available")
+        from shared.local_indexer import build_local_lab_schema
+
+        indexer, index_dir, lab_index_dir, db_path = _make_local_indexer(str(tmp_path))
+        try:
+            folder_path, txt_path, sys_id = _index_small_txt(indexer, str(tmp_path))
+            assert sys_id is not None
+
+            fp_dyn, fp_static, normalize = _make_dummy_callbacks()
+            lab_weights = {"v": 1}
+
+            n_pages = indexer._conn.execute(
+                "SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM local_pages"
+            ).fetchone()["c"]
+            assert n_pages > 0
+
+            def _lab_doc_count() -> int:
+                lab_index = tantivy.Index(build_local_lab_schema(), path=lab_index_dir)
+                lab_index.reload()
+                return lab_index.searcher().num_docs
+
+            for _ in range(2):
+                indexer.build_lab_side_index(
+                    lab_weights=lab_weights,
+                    fingerprint_dyn_fn=fp_dyn,
+                    fingerprint_static_fn=fp_static,
+                    normalize_text_fn=normalize,
+                    lab_schema_version=1,
+                )
+
+            assert _lab_doc_count() == n_pages, (
+                "LAB rebuild must replace, not append — doc count should equal "
+                "page count after repeated rebuilds"
+            )
+        finally:
+            indexer.close()
+
     def test_read_lab_meta_returns_none_when_missing(self, tmp_path):
         """read_lab_meta returns None when .meta.json does not exist."""
         from shared.local_indexer import LocalIndexer

  3593:     def _iterate_lab_source_rows(self) -> Iterator[tuple[str, str, int, int, str]]:
  3594:         """HIGH-4 review fix (option b LOCKED): yield (sys_id, uid, page_num, file_id, content)
  3595:         sourced from the MAIN LOCAL Tantivy stored content field.
  3596: 
  3597:         The SQLite local_files table stores metadata only (no page_text column per Plan 03 +
  3598:         HIGH-4 decision). `local_pages` tracks the (sys_id, uid, page_num) mapping; the page
  3599:         TEXT lives in the main LOCAL Tantivy index at the `content` stored field.
  3600: 
  3601:         Generator steps:
  3602:           1. SELECT (sys_id, uid, page_num, file_id) from local_pages JOIN local_files.
  3603:           2. Open the main LOCAL Tantivy index.
  3604:           3. For each uid: search by unique_id (raw tokenizer), retrieve stored `content`.
  3605:           4. Yield the tuple.
  3606: 
  3607:         Failure modes:
  3608:           - main LOCAL Tantivy missing: log WARNING and yield nothing.
  3609:           - uid present in local_pages but missing from main LOCAL Tantivy: log WARNING, skip.
  3610:         """
  3611:         try:
  3612:             if not os.path.isdir(self._index_dir):
  3613:                 logger.warning(
  3614:                     "HIGH-4: main LOCAL Tantivy missing at %s; LAB rebuild empty",
  3615:                     self._index_dir,
  3616:                 )
  3617:                 return
  3618:             main_schema = build_local_schema()
  3619:             main_index = tantivy.Index(main_schema, path=self._index_dir)

 18328:     def _extract_fl_id(self, res):
 18329:         if not isinstance(res, dict):
 18330:             return None

 18332:         raw_header = res.get('raw_header') or res.get('full_header', '')
 18333:         fl_id = None
 18334: 
 18335:         if raw_header and self.meta_mgr:
 18336:             parsed = self.meta_mgr.parse_full_id_components(raw_header)
 18337:             fl_id = parsed.get('fl_id') or fl_id
 18338: 
 18339:         if not fl_id:

 18341: 
 18342:         if not fl_id:
 18343:             img_field = display.get('img')

 18346:                 if m:
 18347:                     fl_id = m.group(1)
 18348: 
 18349:         if not fl_id:

 18354:                     fl_id = m.group(1)
 18355: 
 18356:         return fl_id

 18358:     def show_full_text(self):
 18359:         row = self.results_table.currentRow()
 18360:         if row < 0: return
 18361: 
 18362:         # currentRow() indexes the FULL table, but _collect_sorted_results() drops
 18363:         # hidden (filtered) rows ג€” using row as an index into that list opens the
 18364:         # wrong result when any earlier row is hidden. Read the clicked row's own
 18365:         # result dict and delegate to the uid-locator so this path stays consistent
 18366:         # with the נ‘ "View result" button (both resolve position by uid).
 18367:         item = self.results_table.item(row, self.COL_SYS_ID)
 18368:         res = item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) if item else None
 18369:         if not res:
 18370:             return
 18371:         # Phase 95 smoke-fix (E): LOCAL double-click ג†’ ResultDialog (shows text).
 18372:         # The Browse panel path was removed because Browse does not render file text
 18373:         # correctly for LOCAL hits. ResultDialog already renders full_text which is
 18374:         # populated for LOCAL hits by _build_local_result_dict. The "Open file"
 18375:         # button is injected into ResultDialog by _inject_open_file_btn_into_rd()
 18376:         # so the user can still launch the source file from there.
 18377:         self.show_full_text_for_result(res)

 18379:     def show_full_text_for_result(self, res):
 18380:         if not res:
 18381:             return
 18382:         sorted_results = self._collect_sorted_results()
 18383:         if not sorted_results:
 18384:             sorted_results = [res]
 18385:         # Locate the clicked result. Prefer an EXACT match ג€” same object or same
 18386:         # per-page uid (unique per result). Only fall back to the manuscript/file
 18387:         # id, and take the FIRST such row. A file's pages all share one display
 18388:         # id, so the old loop (no break on the id branch) landed on the LAST
 18389:         # same-id row: every result of a file opened at that file's last
 18390:         # position and the counter ran backwards by file ("500 of 500",
 18391:         # "486 of 500", ...). See bug report 2026-05-27.
 18392:         res_uid = res.get('uid')
 18393:         res_id = (res.get('display') or {}).get('id')
 18394:         target_index = 0
 18395:         matched = False
 18396:         for idx, candidate in enumerate(sorted_results):
 18397:             if candidate is res or (res_uid and candidate.get('uid') == res_uid):
 18398:                 target_index = idx
 18399:                 matched = True
 18400:                 break
 18401:         if not matched and res_id:
 18402:             for idx, candidate in enumerate(sorted_results):
 18403:                 if (candidate.get('display') or {}).get('id') == res_id:
 18404:                     target_index = idx
 18405:                     break
 18406:         ResultDialog(self, sorted_results, target_index, self.meta_mgr, self.searcher).exec()

   196:     def test_lab_rebuild_after_source_file_deleted(self, tmp_path):

   229:             assert not os.path.exists(txt_path)

   237:                 lab_schema_version=1,

    38: def _make_local_indexer(tmp_dir: str):

    55: def _index_small_txt(indexer, tmp_dir: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    56:     """Write a small TXT file, register its folder, and scan. Returns (folder_path, sys_id)."""
    57:     folder_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "docs")
    58:     os.makedirs(folder_path, exist_ok=True)
    59:     txt_path = os.path.join(folder_path, "sample.txt")
    60:     with open(txt_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    61:         f.write("This is a test document with some Hebrew content.\n")
    62:     indexer.add_folder(folder_path)
    63:     indexer.scan_all()
    64:     # Get the sys_id from SQLite
    65:     row = indexer._conn.execute("SELECT sys_id FROM local_files LIMIT 1").fetchone()
    66:     sys_id = row["sys_id"] if row else None
    67:     return folder_path, txt_path, sys_id

 18437:     def on_search_result_item_changed(self, item):
 18438:         """Handle individual checkbox changes in search results."""
 18439:         if item.column() != self.COL_CHECKBOX:
 18440:             return
 18441: 
 18442:         # Check if all items are checked to sync "Select All"
 18443:         all_checked = True
 18444:         has_selection = False
 18445: 
 18446:         # Avoid full iteration if possible, but we need to check "Select All" status
 18447:         rows = self.results_table.rowCount()
 18448:         # To optimize, we just iterate. Rows are usually < 500.
 18449:         for i in range(rows):
 18450:             if self.results_table.isRowHidden(i):
 18451:                 continue

 18456:                 else:

 18458:

import os, tempfile
try:
    import tantivy
except Exception as exc:
    print(f\"SKIP tantivy import failed: {exc"'!r}")
    raise SystemExit(0)

schema_builder = tantivy.SchemaBuilder()
schema_builder.add_text_field("id", stored=True)
schema = schema_builder.build()
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
    idx = tantivy.Index(schema, path=d)
    w = idx.writer()
    w.add_document(tantivy.Document(id="old"))
    w.commit()
    idx.reload()
    before = idx.searcher().num_docs

    w = idx.writer()
    w.delete_all_documents()
    w.add_document(tantivy.Document(id="new"))
    w.commit()
    idx.reload()
    searcher = idx.searcher()
    docs = []

    for _, addr in searcher.search(q, limit=10).hits:
        docs.append(searcher.doc(addr).get_first("id"))
    print({"before": before, "after": searcher.num_docs, "new_docs": docs})

# Codex Review Brief ג€” My Library post-UAT bugfix batch (2026-05-27)

> **Purpose:** Self-contained brief for an external Codex review AND for a fresh
> Claude session (post-`/clear`) to interpret Codex's findings and apply fixes.
> Everything needed is in this file + commit `e397ad30`. You do NOT need the
> original conversation.

## How to run the review

```bash
codex exec "Review git commit e397ad30 in this repo (GenizahSearch). Read this
brief first: .planning/phases/97.3-my-library-uat-stability/97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-BRIEF.md
Then critique the diff for correctness, edge cases, and regressions. Focus on
the four REVIEW CONCERNS listed in the brief. Report findings by severity
(HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW) with file:line and a concrete fix for each. Be skeptical ג€”
these are untested-in-UI desktop changes." 2>&1 | tee \
.planning/phases/97.3-my-library-uat-stability/97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md
```

(`gemini` CLI is also available as a second opinion. Skip the `claude` CLI ג€” we are Claude.)

## Context

- **Branch:** `phase-98-nli-resilience`  **Commit under review:** `e397ad30`  **Parent:** `c43f3eb9`
- These are THREE interrelated bugs the user (Hillel) hit during live UAT of the
  desktop **My Library** feature (PyQt6 7th tab indexing local .docx/.pdf/.txt/.html/.xlsx/.csv
  into a Tantivy side-index, merged into Search via RRF k=60).
- Dual-app project: `genizah_core.py` (shared engine, ~10K lines), `genizah_app.py`
  (desktop, ~25K lines), `desktop/my_library_tab.py`, `shared/local_indexer.py`.
- The fixes are committed but **NOT yet verified in the running desktop UI**
  (headless test harness can't construct the QWidgets). That's why review matters.

## The three bugs and their fixes

### Bug 1 ג€” Office temp files (`~$foo.docx`) polluting the indexer
**Symptom (from console):**
```
Phase 97 C-05: skipping c:\...\~$׳™ ׳•׳ ׳›׳¨׳™.docx ג€” not a valid zip-container file
```
Word/Excel/PowerPoint create transient lock/owner files named `~$<name>.ext`
while a doc is open. They are not valid zip containers, so the docx/xlsx
zip-bomb pre-check (`shared/local_indexer.py` `_check_zip_bomb`, status path at
~line 1621-1655) logged them as `zip_bomb_suspected`, wrote rows to
`processed_files` + `local_files`, and they showed in the opt-out tree forever.

**Fix:** new `is_office_temp_file(filename)` helper (basename starts with `~$`),
applied at all THREE enumeration sites:
- `shared/local_indexer.py::_iterate_supported_files` (the indexer scan that builds `disk_files`)
- `shared/local_indexer.py::prescan_count` (the D-26 ceiling-dialog counter)
- `desktop/my_library_tab.py::FolderWalkWorker` (the opt-out tree walker; imports the helper)

### Bug 2 ג€” `'SearchEngine' object has no attribute 'dynamic_rank_map'`
**Symptom (from console):**
```
MyLibraryTab._maybe_rebuild_lab_if_stale: rebuild_local_lab_index failed:
'SearchEngine' object has no attribute 'dynamic_rank_map'
```
**Architecture:** there are two engines. `LabEngine` (genizah_core.py class ~line 693)
owns `dynamic_rank_map` + `settings`. `SearchEngine` (class ~line 6769) does NOT ג€”
this same split was already fixed under "CR-01" for `_current_lab_weights_hash`
(SearchEngine version at ~6960 uses `getattr` defaults; LabEngine version at ~808
uses the real attrs). But `SearchEngine.rebuild_local_lab_index` (~7015) still read
`self.dynamic_rank_map`/`self.settings` directly ג†’ crash.

The caller `MyLibraryTab._maybe_rebuild_lab_if_stale` (my_library_tab.py ~1037)
checks freshness on the **LabEngine** (`lab._check_local_lab_freshness()`) but
rebuilt on the **SearchEngine** (`search.rebuild_local_lab_index(...)`).

**Fix:** `rebuild_local_lab_index(self, local_indexer, lab_engine=None)`. Caller
passes `lab_engine=lab`. Weights are sourced from `lab_engine` (falling back to
`self` via `getattr`) and normalized **identically** to
`LabEngine._current_lab_weights_hash`:
```python
lab_weights = {
    "dynamic_rank_map": dyn_map if dyn_map else None,
    "use_dynamic_weights": getattr(settings, "use_dynamic_weights", False) if settings else False,
}
```

**Why the normalization matters (the load-bearing subtlety):**
- `build_lab_side_index` (shared/local_indexer.py ~3550) writes
  `weights_hash = sha256(json.dumps(lab_weights, sort_keys=True))` into `.meta.json`.
- `LabEngine._check_local_lab_freshness` recomputes
  `sha256(json.dumps({dynamic_rank_map if ג€¦ else None, use_dynamic_weights}, sort_keys=True, default=str))`
  and compares to `.meta.json`.
- If these don't match byte-for-byte, the index is judged **perpetually stale**
  and rebuilt on every reload. A naive `getattr(self,ג€¦,None)` fix on SearchEngine
  would have written a None-map hash that never matches the LabEngine's real-map
  hash ג†’ infinite rebuild loop.
- Verified by hand: rebuild's `lab_weights` hash == LabEngine freshness hash for
  both a real JSON map and the None case. (`default=str` is a no-op because the
  map is JSON-native ג€” loaded from `Config.LAB_WEIGHTS_FILE` JSON ג€” and the other
  value is a bool.)

### Bug 3 ג€” LOCAL search result counter "500 of 500", "486 of 500" (backwards)
**Symptom:** A LOCAL search returned 2247 results. Opening a result via the
per-row נ‘ "View result" button showed "result 500 of 500"; the next file "486
of 500"; counting down by file. (Gap of 14 = the previous file had 14 pages.)

**Root cause:** `genizah_app.py::show_full_text_for_result` (~18376, called from
the נ‘ button at ~16697 via `lambda _, r=res: self.show_full_text_for_result(r)`).
The loop that locates the clicked result in `_collect_sorted_results()` fell back
to matching on `display.id` **with no `break`**, so it kept overwriting
`target_index` and landed on the **LAST** row sharing that id. A LOCAL file's
pages all share one `display.id`, so every result of a file opened at that file's
last position ג†’ backwards-by-file counter.

NOTE: source order is correct (Tantivy returns LOCAL hits best-first via
`_query_local_index`, genizah_core ~7054). It was NOT a reversed list.

**Fix:** match exact per-page `uid` first (or object identity), then fall back to
the FIRST `display.id` match with a `break`.

## REVIEW CONCERNS ג€” scrutinize these specifically

1. **Bug 2 hash parity & fingerprint consistency.** Confirm the rebuild's
   `lab_weights` dict serializes to the SAME `weights_hash` that
   `LabEngine._current_lab_weights_hash` computes (note one uses `default=str`,
   the other doesn't ג€” argue why they still match, or find a case where they
   diverge, e.g. a non-JSON-native value ever entering `dynamic_rank_map`). ALSO:
   build-time fingerprints use `dyn_map` from the LabEngine; query-time LAB-mode
   composition uses LabEngine's `dynamic_rank_map`. Confirm these are the same
   object/source so fingerprints are consistent. Is there any caller of
   `rebuild_local_lab_index` that still passes no `lab_engine` and runs on a
   SearchEngine with a real (non-None) `dynamic_rank_map` that we'd now ignore?

2. **Bug 3 `uid` assumption.** Does every LOCAL hit dict actually carry a unique
   per-page `uid`? (See `_build_local_result_dict`, genizah_core ~7117, D-34
   "unique F-suffix per file".) If `uid` is missing/None for some hits, the code
   falls to first-id-match ג€” is landing on the file's FIRST page acceptable when
   the exact page can't be identified? Any case where two DIFFERENT results share
   a `uid`? Does `_collect_sorted_results()` (genizah_app ~18313) preserve `uid`
   on the dicts it returns (it reads `UserRole` data from `COL_SYS_ID`)?

3. **Bug 1 enumeration completeness & deletion semantics.** Are there OTHER file
   enumeration sites that should also skip `~$` (e.g. `estimate_index_size` ג€”
   probably not, it sums index-dir files, not source files)? More important:
   `scan_all` has a "detect deleted files" pass (cached-but-not-on-disk ג†’
   `_delete_file`). A `~$` file indexed BEFORE this fix is now absent from
   `disk_files`, so it will be deleted from the index on next scan. Confirm that's
   harmless cleanup (it is intended), not a data-loss footgun. Case-sensitivity:
   `~$` is ASCII; fine on Windows. Any legitimate user file that starts with `~$`?
   (Effectively never ג€” it's a reserved Office pattern.)

4. **Bug 3 scope.** `show_full_text_for_result` is the נ‘-button path. The
   double-click path is `show_full_text` (~18358) which uses `currentRow()`
   directly and was NOT changed. Confirm the fix doesn't need to be mirrored
   there, and that the two paths stay consistent.

## Already verified (don't re-flag as work to do)
- `ruff check` clean on all 4 files; `ast.parse` clean.
- 25/25 `tests/test_local_lab_invalidation.py` pass (covers `rebuild_local_lab_index` existence + Option-C callback wiring).
- Hand-verified: enumeration skips `~$` (3 real files yielded from a 5-file dir incl. 2 `~$`); weights-hash parity holds for real-map and None.

## Known pre-existing failures (NOT regressions from this commit)
- `tests/test_local_indexer.py::test_supported_file_types_docx_pdf_txt` and
  `::test_unsupported_extension_status` FAIL asserting `.html` ג†’ `"unsupported"`,
  but `.html` extraction was added in Phase 97 (R97.3-N broadened
  `_SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS`). **Confirmed these fail on clean HEAD (parent
  `c43f3eb9`) too.** They are stale tests, out of scope for this batch.
- `tests/test_local_indexer_mutex.py` hangs under the headless harness (pre-existing).

## Deferred (intentionally NOT in this commit ג€” do not "fix")
- The result viewer's denominator reflects only the **currently-loaded table
  batch** (lazy-loaded 500 at a time via `load_next_batch`), so it can read "of
  500" instead of "of 2247" until the user scrolls. Making the dialog span ALL
  results **in the user's custom column-sort order** is a windowed-loading change
  the user (who sorts often) explicitly deferred as "skip if it's a major
  structural change." The Bug 3 fix corrects the *position*; the denominator
  enhancement is a separate future task.

## For the fresh session interpreting Codex output
1. Read `97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md` (Codex findings) alongside this brief and `git show e397ad30`.
2. Triage each finding: real correctness issue vs. style/noise. The four concerns
   above are the high-value areas; weigh HIGH findings there heavily.
3. Apply accepted fixes as NEW commits on `phase-98-nli-resilience` (do not amend
   `e397ad30`). Re-run `ruff` + `tests/test_local_lab_invalidation.py`.
4. After fixes settle, consider adding a `ג… Fixed (2026-05-27)` entry to
   `docs/OPEN_ISSUES.md` and a one-line note in CLAUDE.md "Recently Changed"
   (per project doc-maintenance convention). Not yet done.

   diverge, e.g. a non-JSON-native value ever entering `dynamic_rank_map`). ALSO:
   build-time fingerprints use `dyn_map` from the LabEngine; query-time LAB-mode
   composition uses LabEngine's `dynamic_rank_map`. Confirm these are the same
   object/source so fingerprints are consistent. Is there any caller of
   `rebuild_local_lab_index` that still passes no `lab_engine` and runs on a
   SearchEngine with a real (non-None) `dynamic_rank_map` that we'd now ignore?

2. **Bug 3 `uid` assumption.** Does every LOCAL hit dict actually carry a unique
   per-page `uid`? (See `_build_local_result_dict`, genizah_core ~7117, D-34
   "unique F-suffix per file".) If `uid` is missing/None for some hits, the code
   falls to first-id-match ׳’ג‚¬ג€ is landing on the file's FIRST page acceptable when
   the exact page can't be identified? Any case where two DIFFERENT results share
   a `uid`? Does `_collect_sorted_results()` (genizah_app ~18313) preserve `uid`
   on the dicts it returns (it reads `UserRole` data from `COL_SYS_ID`)?

3. **Bug 1 enumeration completeness & deletion semantics.** Are there OTHER file
   enumeration sites that should also skip `~$` (e.g. `estimate_index_size` ׳’ג‚¬ג€
   probably not, it sums index-dir files, not source files)? More important:
   `scan_all` has a "detect deleted files" pass (cached-but-not-on-disk ׳’ג€ ג€™
   `_delete_file`). A `~$` file indexed BEFORE this fix is now absent from
   `disk_files`, so it will be deleted from the index on next scan. Confirm that's
   harmless cleanup (it is intended), not a data-loss footgun. Case-sensitivity:
   `~$` is ASCII; fine on Windows. Any legitimate user file that starts with `~$`?
   (Effectively never ׳’ג‚¬ג€ it's a reserved Office pattern.)

4. **Bug 3 scope.** `show_full_text_for_result` is the ׳ ֲג€˜ֲ-button path. The
   double-click path is `show_full_text` (~18358) which uses `currentRow()`
   directly and was NOT changed. Confirm the fix doesn't need to be mirrored
   there, and that the two paths stay consistent.

## Already verified (don't re-flag as work to do)
- `ruff check` clean on all 4 files; `ast.parse` clean.
- 25/25 `tests/test_local_lab_invalidation.py` pass (covers `rebuild_local_lab_index` existence + Option-C callback wiring).
- Hand-verified: enumeration skips `~$` (3 real files yielded from a 5-file dir incl. 2 `~$`); weights-hash parity holds for real-map and None.

## Known pre-existing failures (NOT regressions from this commit)
- `tests/test_local_indexer.py::test_supported_file_types_docx_pdf_txt` and
  `::test_unsupported_extension_status` FAIL asserting `.html` ׳’ג€ ג€™ `"unsupported"`,
  but `.html` extraction was added in Phase 97 (R97.3-N broadened
  `_SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS`). **Confirmed these fail on clean HEAD (parent
  `c43f3eb9`) too.** They are stale tests, out of scope for this batch.
- `tests/test_local_indexer_mutex.py` hangs under the headless harness (pre-existing).

## Deferred (intentionally NOT in this commit ׳’ג‚¬ג€ do not "fix")
- The result viewer's denominator reflects only the **currently-loaded table
  batch** (lazy-loaded 500 at a time via `load_next_batch`), so it can read "of
  500" instead of "of 2247" until the user scrolls. Making the dialog span ALL
  results **in the user's custom column-sort order** is a windowed-loading change
  the user (who sorts often) explicitly deferred as "skip if it's a major
  structural change." The Bug 3 fix corrects the *position*; the denominator
  enhancement is a separate future task.

codex
**Findings**

**HIGH** [shared/local_indexer.py:3518](<C:/Genizahsearch/shared/local_indexer.py:3518>)  
`build_lab_side_index()` reuses the existing LOCAL LAB index and only appends documents. Now that `rebuild_local_lab_index()` is actually reachable, a rebuild after weight changes can leave old `fingerprint_dyn` docs beside new ones, and deleted LOCAL pages can remain searchable in LAB. The new `.meta.json` can say ג€freshג€ while the index still contains stale rows.  
Concrete fix: make this a true rebuild: close both SearchEngine/LabEngine LOCAL LAB searchers, build into a temp LAB dir and atomically swap, or at minimum call `writer.delete_all_documents()` before adding current rows and commit once. Add a regression test that rebuilds with changed fingerprints/deleted rows and asserts only current `uid`s remain.

**HIGH** [genizah_core.py:7040](<C:/Genizahsearch/genizah_core.py:7040>) + [genizah_core.py:7005](<C:/Genizahsearch/genizah_core.py:7005>)  
The rebuild writes a LabEngine-derived weights hash, but `SearchEngine._check_local_lab_freshness()` still recomputes from SearchEngineג€™s missing `dynamic_rank_map/settings`, i.e. the ג€no weightsג€ hash. Standard Composition Search uses `SearchEngine.search_composition_logic()` and calls that freshness check at [genizah_core.py:8940](<C:/Genizahsearch/genizah_core.py:8940>), so a freshly rebuilt LabEngine-weighted LOCAL LAB index can be treated as stale and LOCAL LAB hits skipped on that surface.  
Concrete fix: give `SearchEngine` access to the same LabEngine weight source used for rebuild, and use one shared helper to compute the LAB weights dict/hash for `LabEngine`, `SearchEngine`, and `rebuild_local_lab_index()`.

**MEDIUM** [genizah_app.py:18362](<C:/Genizahsearch/genizah_app.py:18362>)  
The eye-button path now locates by `uid`, but double-click still uses `currentRow()` as an index into `_collect_sorted_results()`, which excludes hidden rows. If any earlier rows are hidden by filtering, double-click can open the wrong result while the eye button opens the right one. That leaves the two viewer paths inconsistent.  
Concrete fix: in `show_full_text()`, read the current rowג€™s `COL_SYS_ID` `UserRole` result and delegate to `show_full_text_for_result(res)`, or use the same `uid` locator.

**Notes**

I did not find a Bug 1 deletion footgun for old `~$` zip-bomb rows: the next scan should drop them through the existing cached-not-on-disk delete path. The LOCAL `uid` assumption also looks valid for current hits: `_build_local_result_dict()` preserves `unique_id`, and the table stores the full result dict on `COL_SYS_ID`. I did not run the UI/tests; this was a static review.

The new `.meta.json` can say ג€freshג€ while the index still contains stale rows.  
Concrete fix: make this a true rebuild: close both SearchEngine/LabEngine LOCAL LAB searchers, build into a temp LAB dir and atomically swap, or at minimum call `writer.delete_all_documents()` before adding current rows and commit once. > .planning\phases\97.3-my-library-uat-stability\97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md:4381:.\docs\OPEN_ISSUES.md:70:| **Phase 
97.2: LOCAL recovery cascade — 8 interacting bugs in `shared/local_indexer.py` + `genizah_core.py` + missing Reset My 
Library feature** | `shared/local_indexer.py`, `genizah_core.py`, `desktop/my_library_tab.py`, 
`genizah_translations.py` | ✅ Fixed (2026-05-26) | Phase 97.2 — `Schema error -> LockBusy x3 -> 'Field scan_run_id is 
not defined' -> 'NoneType has no delete_documents'` cascade on the first post-97.1 run, after user stopped a 100K-file 
Dropbox scan mid-run, restarted, and clicked "Remove folder". Root cause: missing `.schema_version` marker check 
(R97.2-F / Bug 6) — Phase 95 installs have `meta.json` but no `.schema_version`, so the existing `actual_marker is not 
None and actual_marker != expected_marker` guard failed to trip the rebuild path on Phase 95 -> Phase 97 upgrade. 
Closed by 8 fixes covering R97.2-A through R97.2-H: R97.2-A redundant `tantivy.Index(...)` reopen deleted at 
`local_indexer.py:1147` + temp-indexer in `genizah_core.py:6878-6896` now closes via `_close_internal_writer_index()` 
in try/finally; R97.2-B explicit null+`gc.collect()` at rebuild swap replaces `del` (Windows weak-del + Rust delayed 
drop + os.rename race); R97.2-C schema-aware Phase 95 fallback in `discard_run` step 2 (probe-based try/except 
ValueError since tantivy-py 0.25.1's Schema lacks `field_names()`); R97.2-D new `LocalIndexerError(RuntimeError)` 
exception class + `_ensure_writer()` fail-loud helper wired at 4 call sites; R97.2-E new 
`LocalIndexer.reset_my_library()` 7-step atomic teardown (close handles -> 7 path-safety pre-checks -> rename-aside 
LOCAL + LAB with rollback -> recreate -> deferred GC via `pending_dir_cleanup` -> reinit -> reload searcher) + new 
"Reset My Library" / "אפס ספריה שלי" toolbar button in `desktop/my_library_tab.py` with destructive red styling, 
proactive active-scan guard, and bilingual two-step typed confirm (`RESET` / `אפס` both accepted); R97.2-F 
schema-marker absence triggers rebuild in both files; R97.2-G null `_del_writer` + `gc.collect()` between discard_run 
steps 2 and 5; R97.2-H short-circuit SQLite delete on Tantivy failure (raises `LocalIndexerError` BEFORE the SQLite 
transaction; prevents orphaned-docs state). 5 RED tests gating the fixes in strict order:

[shared/local_indexer.py:3518](<C:/Genizahsearch/shared/local_indexer.py:3518>)  
  .planning\phases\97.3-my-library-uat-stability\97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md:6272:`build_lab_side_index()` reuses the 
existing LOCAL LAB index and only appends documents. Now that `rebuild_local_lab_index()` is actually reachable, a 
rebuild after weight changes can leave old `fingerprint_dyn` docs beside new ones, and deleted LOCAL pages can remain 
searchable in LAB. The new `.meta.json` can say “fresh” while the index still contains stale rows.  
  .planning\phases\97.3-my-library-uat-stability\97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md:6273:Concrete fix: make this a true 
rebuild: close both SearchEngine/LabEngine LOCAL LAB searchers, build into a temp LAB dir and atomically swap, or at 
minimum call `writer.delete_all_documents()` before adding current rows and commit once. Add a regression test that 
rebuilds with changed fingerprints/deleted rows and asserts only current `uid`s remain.
  .planning\phases\97.3-my-library-uat-stability\97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md:6274:
> .planning\phases\97.3-my-library-uat-stability\97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md:6275:**HIGH** 
[genizah_core.py:7040](<C:/Genizahsearch/genizah_core.py:7040>) + 
[genizah_core.py:7005](<C:/Genizahsearch/genizah_core.py:7005>)  
  .planning\phases\97.3-my-library-uat-stability\97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md:6276:The rebuild writes a 
LabEngine-derived weights hash, but `SearchEngine._check_local_lab_freshness()` still recomputes from SearchEngine’s 
missing `dynamic_rank_map/settings`, i.e. the “no weights” hash. Standard Composition Search uses 
`SearchEngine.search_composition_logic()` and calls that freshness check at 
[genizah_core.py:8940](<C:/Genizahsearch/genizah_core.py:8940>), so a freshly rebuilt LabEngine-weighted LOCAL LAB 
index can be treated as stale and LOCAL LAB hits skipped on that surface.  
  .planning\phases\97.3-my-library-uat-stability\97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md:6277:Concrete fix: give `SearchEngine` 
access to the same LabEngine weight source used for rebuild, and use one shared helper to compute the LAB weights 
dict/hash for `LabEngine`, `SearchEngine`, and `rebuild_local_lab_index()`.

  .planning\phases\97.3-my-library-uat-stability\97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md:6293:`build_lab_side_index()` reuses the 
existing LOCAL LAB index and only appends documents. Now that `rebuild_local_lab_index()` is actually reachable, a 
rebuild after weight changes can leave old `fingerprint_dyn` docs beside new ones, and deleted LOCAL pages can remain 
searchable in LAB. The new `.meta.json` can say “fresh” while the index still contains stale rows.  
  .planning\phases\97.3-my-library-uat-stability\97.3-POSTUAT-CODEX-OUTPUT.md:6294:Concrete fix: make this a true 
rebuild: close both SearchEngine/LabEngine LOCAL LAB searchers, build into a temp LAB dir and atomically swap, or at 
minimum call `writer.delete_all_documents()` before adding current rows and commit once. Add a regression test that 
rebuilds with changed fingerprints/deleted rows and asserts only current `uid`s remain.

  7015:     def rebuild_local_lab_index(self, local_indexer, lab_engine=None) -> None:
  7016:         """Trigger LOCAL LAB rebuild via LocalIndexer, passing fingerprint helpers
  7017:         as callbacks (W5 ג€” Option C LOCKED). Called from MyLibraryTab Refresh
  7018:         (Plan 07) and Toolsג†’Rebuild LAB (per D-38).
  7019: 
  7020:         The three bound-method callbacks (_compute_fingerprint_dyn,
  7021:         _compute_fingerprint_static, _normalize_text) are thin wrappers around
  7022:         the existing text_to_fingerprint / lab_index_normalize helpers in this class.
  7023: 
  7024:         The LAB weights (``dynamic_rank_map`` + ``settings``) live on
  7025:         :class:`LabEngine`, NOT on :class:`SearchEngine` (same split CR-01 fixed
  7026:         for ``_current_lab_weights_hash``). The caller passes the live LabEngine
  7027:         as ``lab_engine`` so the weights ג€” and therefore the build-time
  7028:         fingerprints and the ``weights_hash`` written into ``.meta.json`` ג€”
  7029:         match what the LabEngine freshness check recomputes. Falling back to
  7030:         ``self`` with ``getattr`` defaults keeps a plain SearchEngine from
  7031:         raising ``AttributeError``; sourcing weights from ``self`` (None map)
  7032:         would make the freshness check see a different hash and judge the index
  7033:         perpetually stale.
  7034:         """
  7035:         weights_source = lab_engine if lab_engine is not None else self
  7036:         dyn_map = getattr(weights_source, "dynamic_rank_map", None)
  7037:         settings = getattr(weights_source, "settings", None)
  7038:         # Normalize identically to LabEngine._current_lab_weights_hash so the
  7039:         # weights_hash written here matches the freshness check exactly.
  7040:         lab_weights = {
  7041:             "dynamic_rank_map": dyn_map if dyn_map else None,
  7042:             "use_dynamic_weights": (
  7043:                 getattr(settings, "use_dynamic_weights", False)
  7044:                 if settings is not None else False
  7045:             ),
  7046:         }
  7047:         local_indexer.build_lab_side_index(
  7048:             lab_weights=lab_weights,

  7050:             fingerprint_static_fn=self._compute_fingerprint_static,
  7051:             normalize_text_fn=self._normalize_text,

  7058:     def _compute_fingerprint_dyn(self, content: str, dynamic_rank_map) -> str:
  7059:         """Compute fingerprint_dyn for a content string using the given rank map.
  7060:         W5 Option C callback ג€” wraps text_to_fingerprint with dynamic weights."""
  7061:         return text_to_fingerprint(content, freq_map=dynamic_rank_map)
  7062: 
  7063:     def _compute_fingerprint_static(self, content: str) -> str:
  7064:         """Compute static fingerprint for a content string using HEBREW_FREQ.
  7065:         W5 Option C callback ג€” wraps text_to_fingerprint with static weights."""
  7066:         return text_to_fingerprint(content, freq_map=HEBREW_FREQ)

  7068:     def _normalize_text(self, content: str) -> str:

codex
**Findings**

**MEDIUM** [shared/local_indexer.py](<C:/Genizahsearch/shared/local_indexer.py:3544>)  
`delete_all_documents()` is queued before the rebuild proves it can produce a complete replacement. `_iterate_lab_source_rows()` can yield nothing when the main LOCAL Tantivy index cannot open, and per-row read/fingerprint failures are swallowed, but [writer.commit()](<C:/Genizahsearch/shared/local_indexer.py:3571>) still publishes the cleared/partial LAB index and then writes fresh `.meta.json`. This is not a Tantivy ordering problem: delete-all plus add in one commit should not delete the freshly added docs. The risk is application-level partial publish.

Concrete fix: make the rebuild all-or-nothing. Count expected current rows from `local_pages JOIN local_files WHERE pending_delete = 0`; if source open fails or `rows_written != expected`, call `writer.rollback()`, do not write `.meta.json`, and surface/log a rebuild failure. Stronger fix: build in a temp LAB dir and atomically swap only after validating the UID set.

**LOW** [tests/test_local_lab_invalidation.py](<C:/Genizahsearch/tests/test_local_lab_invalidation.py:351>)  
`test_lab_rebuild_is_replace_not_append` proves the simple duplicate-append case: two identical rebuilds would produce `2 * n_pages` without the fix, so the doc-count assertion is useful. It does not prove the “deleted pages no longer searchable” half of the original finding, nor that the surviving LAB docs are exactly the current `local_pages` UIDs. A wrong replacement with the same cardinality could pass.

Concrete fix: extend the test to compare `unique_id` values in LAB against `SELECT uid FROM local_pages`, and add a second scenario that calls `_delete_file(...)`, rebuilds LAB, and asserts the deleted UID is absent.

**Notes**

I do not see a concrete regression in the double-click change. Normal rows store `res` on `COL_SYS_ID` at population time, and double-click now delegates to the same `show_full_text_for_result(res)` path as the eye button. Empty/no-current-row and missing-`UserRole` cases return cleanly; hidden rows are not clickable, and the delegated path still locates within visible rows.

I did not re-flag the deferred SearchEngine LAB weights-hash mismatch. I could not run pytest here: `pytest` is not on PATH, and both repo virtualenv Python launchers point to missing local Python installs.
